*    SOLUTION FOR EVERY STRIPPED BOLT - our comprehensive fastener extraction kits provide full-spectrum solutions for damaged fasteners in automotive repair scenarios. *    BITE HARDER & HOLD STRONGER - the product's thread structure has been upgraded with optimized engagement geometry, ensuring superior interlocking with damaged fasteners. This enhancement effectively resolves slippage risks during extraction while improving operational efficiency and extending service life;

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

### Customers say

Customers find the bolt extractor set to be of above-average quality and well worth the money, with one customer noting it worked extremely well on an oil pan drain bolt. The tool is easy to use, taking off bolts with zero effort, and customers find it very handy. While some customers report the set is durable, others mention it broke off right away on the first turn. The biting and grip features receive mixed feedback, with customers noting the sockets bite hard but the tips aren't as sharp or strong as anticipated.

_5 out of 5 stars_

##### Best extractor ever!

I spent roughly 50 hours trying all kinds of ways to get two horribly mangled bolts off, including welding, other extractors, hammering, and cutting. These did the trick right away. Wish I tried it first. How to use: Spray penetrating oil on the bolt head and threads. I used PB Blaster, Kroil, and CRC Freeze-Off...yes, all three, one after another. I used an automatic center punch to get a hole as close to center as I could, which was 30 degrees off-center. I used a good drill bit and drilled through for at least five seconds, staying as steady and centered as possible. I got the biggest size that could poke into the hole and hammered it in. Not as powerful as possible, but not just tapping either. About five medium swings. Then I carefully got a long non-flexing ratchet onto the extractor. Use extensions or adapters if necessary. I suggest using a ratchet, not a breaker bar, so you can be sure to only use a loosening motion, and so you can twist the head before attaching it to the extractor's socket. Keep turning without letting go, as it will bite into the bolt only via the loosening motion. You'll start to feel it engage after about a quarter-turn. Keep going as centered and steady as possible. Since I was a little off-center, I had to switch ratchets for a different fit after a turn or so. Take care not to apply any tightening motion if you also have to switch. Eventually it will come off easily. Then feel a great sense of relief triumph.

[![Image 152](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif) gaamoo](https://www.amazon.com/gp/profile/amzn1.account.AHT6TSRV5DEBTNW575BHJHTFMFAQ?ref=cm_cr_dp_d_bdcrb_top) _5 out of 5 stars_ ##### [Best extractor ever!](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/srp/-/R1RJ049YZM6YHQ/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?_encoding=UTF8&ie=UTF8)Reviewed in the United States on May 26, 2026 [Size: 15pcs](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/B0DY7WTMR8/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format)[Verified Purchase](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?ie=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)  Brief content visible, double tap to read full content. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. I spent roughly 50 hours trying all kinds of ways to get two horribly mangled bolts off, including welding, other extractors, hammering, and cutting. These did the trick right away. Wish I tried it first.

How to use: Spray penetrating oil on the bolt head and threads. I used PB Blaster, Kroil, and CRC Freeze-Off...yes, all three, one after another. I used an automatic center punch to get a hole as close to center as I could, which was 30 degrees off-center. I used a good drill bit and drilled through for at least five seconds, staying as steady and centered as possible. I got the biggest size that could poke into the hole and hammered it in. Not as powerful as possible, but not just tapping either. About five medium swings. Then I carefully got a long non-flexing ratchet onto the extractor. Use extensions or adapters if necessary. I suggest using a ratchet, not a breaker bar, so you can be sure to only use a loosening motion, and so you can twist the head before attaching it to the extractor's socket. Keep turning without letting go, as it will bite into the bolt only via the loosening motion. You'll start to feel it engage after about a quarter-turn. Keep going as centered and steady as possible. Since I was a little off-center, I had to switch ratchets for a different fit after a turn or so. Take care not to apply any tightening motion if you also have to switch. Eventually it will come off easily. Then feel a great sense of relief triumph.       [Read more Read less](javascript:void(0))

## About this Item

*   SOLUTION FOR EVERY STRIPPED BOLT - our comprehensive fastener extraction kits provide full-spectrum solutions for damaged fasteners in automotive repair scenarios. The system covers all required kit configurations and sizes, ensuring 100% problem resolution for both DIY enthusiasts and professional technicians;
*   BITE HARDER & HOLD STRONGER - the product's thread structure has been upgraded with optimized engagement geometry, ensuring superior interlocking with damaged fasteners. This enhancement effectively resolves slippage risks during extraction while improving operational efficiency and extending service life;
*   HARDER THAN YOUR TOUGHEST BOLT - the product utilizes Cr-Mo steel construction to ensure structural integrity, effectively preventing deformation or secondary damage during fastener extraction. Enhanced with phosphating and blackening surface treatment, this corrosion-resistant finish significantly extends service life;
*   PACKAGE CONTAIN - Bolt Extractor:1/4",5/16"-8,9,3/8",10,7/16"-11,12,1/2",13,9/16"-14,15,5/8"-16,17,11/16",18,3/4"-19. Multi-Spline Screw Extractor:1/8",5/32",3/16",7/32",1/4",9/32",5/16",11/32",3/8",13/32",7/16",15/32",1/2". Hand Extractor:#1,#2,#3,#4,#5,#6. Deep Bolt Extractor:17,19,21,22. Diamond Tip Phillips Bit:PH1,PH2*2,PH3. 3/8"-1/2” Adapter. 3/8" Hex Adapter. Knockout Pin;
*   WARRANTY - as a responsible company deeply committed to the tools category,we will always stand by our Bolt Extractor Kit, guaranteed for ONE YEAR. if you experience any quality issues or accidental damage within the warranty period, please contact us for replacement parts.

## Product Description

 toolant 16pcs Bolt Extractor Kit, 13pcs Screw Extractor Set, 4pcs Lug Nut Removal Tool, 6pcs Hand Extractor Set, 4pcs Diamond Coated Screwdriver Bit, 2pcs Hex Adapter, Knockout Pin
